What the manuscript says

1° Salerno Francesco, di anni ventitré, celibe, nato in Castrolibero, residente in Rende, figlio di Michele, residente in Rende, e di Palermo Maria, residente in detto luogo;

2° Chiappetta Filomena, di anni sedici, nubile, nata in Rende, residente in Rende, figlia di Giovanni, residente in Rende, e di Massei Angela, residente in Rende.

Al presente matrimonio hanno dato il loro consenso Salerno Michele e Palermo Maria, genitori dello sposo, e Chiappetta Giovanni e Massei Angela, genitori della sposa.

Translation

In the year eighteen hundred and ninety-four, on the first day of April, at half past ten in the morning, at the town hall of Rende, open to the public. Before me, Lo Celso Giuseppe, senior assessor standing in for the mayor being indisposed, Registrar of the Civil Status, in official dress, there appeared in person:

1st SALERNO FRANCESCO, aged twenty-three, unmarried, born at Castrolibero, resident at Rende, son of Michele, resident at Rende, and of Palermo Maria, resident in the said place.

2nd CHIAPPETTA FILOMENA, aged sixteen, unmarried, born at Rende, resident at Rende, daughter of Giovanni, resident at Rende, and of Massei Angela, resident at Rende.

Who requested me to unite them in marriage. To that end they presented me with a single document, the certificate of the banns celebrated in this comune on Sundays the fourth and the eleventh of March of the current year.

The articles one hundred and thirty, one hundred and thirty-one and one hundred and thirty-two of the Civil Code having been read to the spouses, and each having answered in the affirmative, I pronounced in the name of the law that they are united in marriage.

Witnesses: Rendano Alfonso, aged thirty-five, landholder; and Vercillo Salvatore, aged forty-five, farmer.

To the present marriage their consent was given by SALERNO MICHELE and PALERMO MARIA, the husband's parents, and CHIAPPETTA GIOVANNI and MASSEI ANGELA, the wife's parents.

What you need to know about this record

  • El consentimiento de los padres no es una fórmula: el Código Civil lo exigía para los varones menores de veinticinco años, y Francesco tenía veintitrés. Michele y Maria Antonia tuvieron que comparecer en persona.
  • Es el rastro más tardío que tenemos de Michele: el expósito del torno estaba vivo el 1 de abril de 1894, con unos 69 años, viendo casarse a su hijo. Su defunción es posterior a esa fecha.
  • Los novios no firman «per essere analfabeti come hanno dichiarato». Firman los dos testigos y el oficial.
  • Los processetti de 1894 NO existen: esa serie en Rende acaba en 1865, con el fondo de la Restauración. Y el propio acta dice que se presentó un solo documento.

Cómo se localizó
por el índice alfabético del final del registro (página 225 de 228), que da siete Pizzi: Nicola 17, ANGIOLINA 65, Amalia 81, Bambina 90, Filomena Giuditta 266, Filomena 280 y Rocco 312. Ninguna «Angela»

What the manuscript says

Numero 65 — Angiolina Pizzi
L'anno mille ottocento sessantanove, il giorno dodici del mese di Febbrajo, nella
Casa Comunale di Atessa, alle ore sedici. Avanti di me Giuseppe Falcucci Sindaco,
ed uffiziale dello Stato civile del Comune di Atessa, Circondario di Vasto,
Provincia di Chieti, è comparso DOMENICO PIZZI fu Giuseppe, d'anni trentanove,
muratore, domiciliato in Atessa nel rione del Castello, il quale mi ha presentato
una bambina di sesso femminile, che dichiara essergli nata il dì sopradetto, alle
ore otto italiane, da sua moglie TERESA FLOCCO fu Pasquale, d'anni trentacinque,
filatrice, seco lui domiciliata, alla quale figlia dichiara di dare il nome di
ANGIOLINA.
[al margen] nel 1892 al N. 101 sposò Nicola Faienza — 26 agosto 93

Translation

In the year eighteen hundred and sixty-nine, on the TWELFTH day of the month of FEBRUARY, at the Town Hall of Atessa, at SIXTEEN HOURS.

Before me, GIUSEPPE FALCUCCI, Mayor and registrar of the Civil Status of the comune of ATESSA, District of VASTO, Province of CHIETI, there appeared DOMENICO PIZZI, SON OF THE LATE GIUSEPPE, aged THIRTY-NINE, STONEMASON, resident at Atessa in the QUARTER OF THE CASTELLO.

Who presented to me A GIRL of the female sex, whom he declares was born to him ON THE DAY AFORESAID, at EIGHT HOURS ITALIAN STYLE, OF HIS WIFE TERESA FLOCCO, DAUGHTER OF THE LATE PASQUALE, aged THIRTY-FIVE, SPINNER, resident with him; upon which daughter he declares he bestows the name ANGIOLINA.

The said declaration was made in the presence of FRANCESCO MARCUCCI (uncertain reading), son of the late Giuseppantonio, aged fifty, and of ALBERTO FARINA (uncertain reading), son of the late Domenico, aged thirty-six, farmhands resident at Atessa, witnesses chosen by the informant; to whom I read the present record, drawn up at the same time in both original registers, and signed by no one but me AND THE INFORMANT, the witnesses having stated that they could not write. — Domenico Pizzi. — The Registrar of the Civil Status, Giuseppe Falcucci.

IN THE MARGIN, in another hand: «In 1892, under number 101, she married NICOLA FAIENZA» — a note dated 26 August 1893.

What you need to know about this record

  • SE LLAMA ANGIOLINA, NO ANGELA. El acta le impone «Angiolina» y el índice del registro la recoge así. «Angela» es como la nombra la memoria familiar. Es la misma persona: lo prueban los padres.
  • NACIÓ EL 12 DE FEBRERO, NO EL 17. El bloque familiar sin fuentes daba el 17 de febrero de 1869. El acta dice «il giorno DODICI del mese di Febbrajo» y que la niña nació «il dì sopradetto», ese mismo día. Cinco días de error, y son cinco días que confirman lo que ya se sabía de ese bloque: los datos están, pero no son fiables al detalle.
  • LOS PADRES CUADRAN EXACTAMENTE. «Domenico Pizzi FU GIUSEPPE, muratore» y «Teresa Flocco FU PASQUALE, filatrice». Es la prueba que se buscaba, y de propina rescata dos abuelos: Giuseppe Pizzi y Pasquale Flocco, los dos ya muertos en febrero de 1869.
  • LA NOTA AL MARGEN ES EL HALLAZGO GRANDE: «nel 1892 al N. 101 SPOSÒ NICOLA FAIENZA». La boda con el apellido Faienza deja de ser un dato de un árbol ajeno y pasa a estar escrita al margen de una partida original. Y dice dónde: la anotación está en el registro de ATESSA, con número de acta del propio municipio, así que el matrimonio se celebró EN ATESSA en 1892 (acta 101) y no en Paglieta.
  • CONSECUENCIA INMEDIATA PARA BUSCAR A DOMENICO FAIENZA: si sus padres se casaron en Atessa en 1892 y la familia de ella es de Atessa de varias generaciones, el nacimiento del hijo hay que buscarlo en ATESSA, no en Paglieta. El índice de Paglieta 1899, que se ha leído entero, no tiene ni un Faienza.
  • EL PADRE FIRMÓ. El formulario acaba «e non firmato che da me, E DAL DICHIARANTE, avendo asserito i testimoni di non saper scrivere», y debajo está la firma «Domenico Pizzi». Un albañil que sabe escribir en 1869, con dos testigos analfabetos al lado. En toda la rama calabresa de este árbol no hay una sola firma de un antepasado.
  • «ORE OTTO ITALIANE»: el acta usa todavía la hora italiana, contada desde el atardecer. En Atessa, a mediados de febrero, ocho horas después de la puesta de sol son aproximadamente las dos de la madrugada.
Spouse

Nicola Faienza

A farmhand from Atessa, born around 1869. He married Angiolina Pizzi on 29 December 1892, record 101 of the Atessa register. Until August 2026 absolutely nothing was known about him; the marriage record says it all at once: where he was born, what he did and whose son he was.

No record obtained yet

RESUELTO EN AGOSTO DE 2026 CON EL ACTA 101 DE ATESSA, que está transcrita entera en la ficha del matrimonio. La cadena fue ésta: al margen de la partida de nacimiento de Angiolina Pizzi (Atessa, 1869, acta 65) una mano posterior anotó «nel 1892 al N. 101 sposò NICOLA FAIENZA», con fecha de 26 de agosto de 1893. Con el año, el número y el municipio se pidió el documento, y el documento contestó a todo. LO QUE DICE: que tenía VEINTITRÉS AÑOS —o sea que nació hacia 1869, el mismo año que su mujer—, que era CONTADINO, que había NACIDO EN ATESSA y vivía en Atessa, y que era hijo de GIUSEPPE FAIENZA, contadino de Atessa y vivo entonces, y de la difunta MARIA STANISCIA. Como no llegaba a los veinticinco años, el Código Civil exigía el consentimiento de su padre, y el acta lo hace constar: «ha prestato il consenso il padre dello sposo». Ese trámite es, de paso, la prueba de que Giuseppe Faienza estaba vivo y localizable en Atessa en diciembre de 1892. Y LO QUE TUMBA: la pista de PAGLIETA, definitivamente. Nicola no era de Paglieta ni de ningún otro sitio: era de Atessa, como su padre y como su mujer. Aquélla venía del mismo bloque familiar sin una sola cita que ya había errado antes, y con ésta van tres veces comprobadas. LO QUE FALTA: su propia partida de nacimiento, en los Nati de Atessa hacia 1869, y la de su hijo Domenico, que no está en Atessa 1899 —el índice del año se ha leído entero— y habrá que buscar en 1900.

NUEVO EN AGOSTO DE 2026, y es el primer Faienza de una generación anterior a Nicola que aparece escrito en un documento. Sale del acta de matrimonio de su hijo (Atessa, 29 de diciembre de 1892, acta 101, transcrita en la ficha del matrimonio): «NICOLA FAIENZA… figlio di GIUSEPPE, contadino, residente in Atessa». QUE ESTABA VIVO NO ES UNA DEDUCCIÓN DEL FORMULARIO, ES UN HECHO DEL ACTA. Nicola tenía veintitrés años y el Código Civil exigía consentimiento paterno por debajo de los veinticinco; el acta anota que «ha prestato il consenso il padre dello sposo, come risulta ed è spiegato dall'atto di richiesta delle pubblicazioni». Alguien fue a declararlo ante el oficial en diciembre de 1892. LO QUE NO SE SABE: cuándo nació, de quién era hijo y cuándo murió. Su matrimonio con Maria Staniscia sería de hacia 1865 y estaría en los Matrimoni de Atessa; su defunción, en los Morti de Atessa posteriores a 1892. Conviene tener presente que en Atessa hay varias familias Faienza a la vez —en los nacimientos de 1899 aparecen tres distintas, la de Michele fu Carmine y las de Antonio y Vincenzo di Pasquale—, así que el apellido solo no basta: hará falta el nombre del padre en cada acta.

NUEVA EN AGOSTO DE 2026. El acta de matrimonio de su hijo Nicola (Atessa, 29 de diciembre de 1892, acta 101) dice «e della FU MARIA STANISCIA, residente in vita in Atessa». El «fu» y el «in vita» dicen lo mismo dos veces: en diciembre de 1892 ya estaba muerta, y mientras vivió vivió en Atessa. Es la tercera madre seguida que las actas de Atessa se molestan en nombrar —después de Santa Menna en 1841, Rosa Iacobitti en 1847 y Antonia di Iorio en 1891—, frente a los cuatro formularios gemelos de Rende que dejaron el hueco en blanco cuatro veces. STANISCIA es apellido corriente en Atessa: el índice de matrimonios de 1892 trae varios, y en los nacimientos de 1899 vuelve a salir. Su defunción tendría que estar en los Morti de Atessa anteriores a 1892.

Año manuscrito
«mille ottocento novantadue» — coincide con el catálogo
Registrar
Francesco Falcucci, assessore municipale delegado por el sindaco «con atto del venticinque novembre milleottocentottantanove, debitamente approvato»
Cómo se localizó
el número de acta ya se sabía por la nota al margen del acta 65 de 1869. El índice alfabético va en las páginas 135 a 138 y el matrimonio más alto que indexa es el 104. La página 110 es la portada de la PARTE II, así que la parte I ocupa las páginas 1 a 109 a razón de una acta por imagen; un sondeo en la página 60 dio el acta 58, o sea acta = página − 2, y la 103 dio la 101 a la primera. Cuatro imágenes en total

What the manuscript says

Numero 101 — Faienza Nicola / Angiolina Pizzi

L'anno mille ottocento novantadue, addì ventinove di Dicembre, a ore
antimeridiane undici e minuti quindici, nella Casa Comunale di Atessa, aperto al
pubblico. Avanti di me Francesco Falcucci Assessore Municipale delegato dal
Sindaco con atto del venticinque novembre milleottocentottantanove, debitamente
approvato, Ufficiale dello Stato Civile, vestito in forma ufficiale, sono
personalmente comparsi:

1. NICOLA FAIENZA, di anni ventitré, contadino, nato in Atessa, residente in
Atessa, figlio di GIUSEPPE, contadino, residente in Atessa, e della fu MARIA
STANISCIA, residente in vita in Atessa;

2. ANGIOLINA PIZZI, di anni ventitré, donna di casa, nata in Atessa, residente in
Atessa, figlia del fu DOMENICO, residente in vita in Atessa, e di TERESA FLOCCO,
residente in Atessa…

…A questo atto sono stati presenti: Luzio Iannone, di anni ventidue, scrivano, e
Emidio Flocco, di anni cinquantasei, caffettiere, entrambi residenti in questo
Comune. Il documento presentato è il certificato delle pubblicazioni eseguite da
me Uffiziale dello Stato Civile di Atessa, cioè la prima nel dì diciotto e la
seconda nel dì venticinque volgente Dicembre. Al suddetto matrimonio ha prestato
il consenso IL PADRE DELLO SPOSO, come risulta ed è spiegato dall'atto di
richiesta delle pubblicazioni. — (a) le parole interlineate «vita in» valgano per
non scritte. Letto il presente atto a tutti gl'intervenuti, gli sposi ed i
testimoni si sono meco sottoscritti.

Faienza Nicola · Angiolina Pizzi · Luzio Iannone testimone · Emidio Flocco
testimone · L'Ufficiale dello Stato Civile, Francesco Falcucci

Translation

In the year eighteen hundred and ninety-two, on the TWENTY-NINTH OF DECEMBER, at a QUARTER PAST ELEVEN in the morning, at the town hall of ATESSA, open to the public.

Before me, FRANCESCO FALCUCCI, municipal assessor delegated by the mayor by act of the twenty-fifth of November eighteen hundred and eighty-nine, duly approved, Registrar of the Civil Status, in official dress, there appeared in person:

1st NICOLA FAIENZA, aged TWENTY-THREE, FARMHAND, BORN AT ATESSA, resident at Atessa, SON OF GIUSEPPE, farmhand, resident at Atessa, AND OF THE LATE MARIA STANISCIA, who in life resided at Atessa.

2nd ANGIOLINA PIZZI, aged TWENTY-THREE, housewife, born at Atessa, resident at Atessa, DAUGHTER OF THE LATE DOMENICO, who in life resided at Atessa, AND OF TERESA FLOCCO, resident at Atessa.

Who requested me to unite them in marriage. To that end they presented me with a single document; the articles one hundred and thirty, one hundred and thirty-one and one hundred and thirty-two of the Civil Code having been read to the spouses, and having asked the husband whether he wishes to take as his wife the ANGIOLINA PIZZI here present, and her whether she wishes to take as her husband the NICOLA FAIENZA here present, and each having answered in the affirmative, I pronounced in the name of the law that they are united in marriage.

Witnesses: LUZIO IANNONE, aged twenty-two, CLERK, and EMIDIO FLOCCO, aged fifty-six, CAFÉ KEEPER, both resident in this comune.

The document presented is the certificate of the banns made by myself as Registrar of the Civil Status of Atessa, the first on the EIGHTEENTH and the second on the TWENTY-FIFTH of the current December.

To the said marriage consent was given by THE HUSBAND'S FATHER, as appears and is set out in the act requesting the banns.

(a) The interlined words «vita in» are to be taken as not written.

The present record having been read to all present, the spouses and the witnesses signed it with me.

What you need to know about this record

  • ERA EL CABO MÁS CORTO Y MÁS RENTABLE QUE HABÍA ABIERTO, Y HA PAGADO. De Nicola Faienza no se sabía nada en absoluto: ni la edad, ni el oficio, ni el pueblo, ni los padres. El acta da las cuatro cosas en un solo renglón.
  • MATA LA PISTA DE PAGLIETA DEFINITIVAMENTE. «Nato in Atessa, residente in Atessa, figlio di Giuseppe, contadino, residente in Atessa». Ni el novio ni su padre eran de Paglieta. Aquel pueblo venía del bloque familiar sin fuentes, y con ésta van tres veces que ese bloque falla en una comprobación directa.
  • AÑADE UNA GENERACIÓN DE FAIENZA: Giuseppe Faienza y Maria Staniscia, los padres del novio, que hasta hoy no existían en ningún papel. Él vivo en 1892, ella ya muerta.
  • EL CONSENTIMIENTO PATERNO NO ES FÓRMULA. El Código Civil lo exigía a los varones menores de veinticinco años y Nicola tenía veintitrés, igual que le pasó a Francesco Salerno en Rende dos años después. Que conste por escrito es la prueba de que Giuseppe Faienza estaba vivo y a mano en diciembre de 1892.
  • LOS DOS NOVIOS SABÍAN ESCRIBIR, y eso en estos registros es excepcional: la fórmula habitual de la época es «essendo i testimoni analfabeti». Aquí firman los cuatro. El padre de ella, Domenico Pizzi, también firmaba: lo hizo en 1869 al declarar el nacimiento de esta misma novia.
  • CONFIRMA A LA NOVIA PUNTO POR PUNTO: veintitrés años (nació el 12 de febrero de 1869), hija del difunto Domenico (murió el 28 de septiembre de 1891) y de Teresa Flocco, que seguía viva. La corrección (a) al pie tacha el «in vita» que el escribano había interlineado sobre ella: no era una viuda más, estaba viva.
  • CORRIGE UNA LECTURA ANTERIOR. La delegación del alcalde en Francesco Falcucci se anotó en la ficha del acta de 1891 como «24 de noviembre de 1889». Aquí se lee VENTICINQUE, y los nacimientos de Atessa de 1899 repiten «venticinque». Era el 25.
  • SE LOCALIZÓ EN CUATRO IMÁGENES Y A LA PRIMERA en la última: el número lo daba la nota al margen de 1869, y la portada de la PARTE II en la página 110 dejaba la aritmética servida.
  • LO QUE ABRE: la partida de nacimiento del propio Nicola, en los Nati de Atessa hacia 1869 —el mismo registro y casi el mismo año que el de su mujer—, y el matrimonio de sus padres, hacia 1865.
Sibling

Giuseppe Salerno

The first son. Born at Rende in 1895; he was 24 in 1919.

Child of Francesco Salerno · Filomena Chiappetta

The first of nine

He was born on 23 February 1895 at ten in the morning, in the contrada Saporito at Rende. His father registered him the next day at the same hour, before Spina Raffaele, secretary delegated by the mayor. The witnesses were Zicarelli Carmine, a twenty-eight-year-old day labourer, and Marchese Vincenzo, a sixty-year-old farmer.

On that record Francesco declared thirty years. He was twenty-four. It is the first in a run of misdeclared ages that has forced every record of this family to be checked by the mother's name and the sequence number, and never by the father's age.

He is the first child of Francesco and Filomena, born eleven months after the wedding. In 1919 he was twenty-four and was among those who crossed to Argentina — though the year of the voyage is still not settled: Italy did not allow men of military age to emigrate until after the war, and emigration to Argentina did not resume until March 1920.

Translation

In the year eighteen hundred and ninety-five, on the twenty-fourth day of February, at ten in the morning, at the town hall, before Spina Raffaele, secretary delegated by act of the mayor of the sixteenth of December eighteen hundred and ninety-four, there appeared SALERNO FRANCESCO, farmer, resident at Rende.

He declares that of CHIAPPETTA FILOMENA, housewife, his wife living with him, there was born on the twenty-third of the current month, at ten in the morning, in the house situated in contrada Saporito, a boy, upon whom he bestows the name GIUSEPPE.

What you need to know about this record

  • La edad declarada del padre (30) es errónea: Francesco había nacido el 7 de diciembre de 1870, luego tenía 24. Las edades declaradas en estos registros son aproximadas.
  • El bloque de texto de arriba a la derecha de esa misma página —un acuerdo de ama de cría con Bruno Maria— NO pertenece a esta acta, sino a la 42.
Sibling

Antonio Salerno

In the records: ANTHONY SALERNO en Estados Unidos

The brother who went to Chicago. Second son of Francesco and Filomena, born at Rende in 1896; he boarded at Naples at seventeen and landed in New York on 16 April 1914. He died in Illinois in 1981, aged eighty-four, after sixty-seven years in the same town and thirty-odd in the same feed mill. He is by far the brother of Settimo about whom most is known.

Child of Francesco Salerno · Filomena Chiappetta

Married Rosina Salerno

Parent of Domenico Salerno · Filomena Salerno

The record that exposed the catalogue

He was born on 13 June 1896 in the contrada S. Agostino at Rende and was registered the following day at seven in the morning. His father declared twenty-five years, and this time the figure was exact. The witnesses were two village farmers: Greco Pasquale and Serpe Serafino.

That exactness solved a serious problem. The register holding his record, Rende's number 87, is listed in the archive catalogue as «Births 1895», but the handwritten year in the heading says «novantasei» quite clearly and the father's age fits 1896. From this it emerged that the catalogue's dating is not reliable and that the Rende birth volumes do not run one per year: they split years in two. Since then there has been a rule in this investigation — read the handwritten year before accepting any record.

The family had moved from Saporito, where Giuseppe had been born, to S. Agostino, the contrada where they would live again from 1904.

Family memory places him in Chicago, alongside a Vincenzo. There is as yet no document to support it: it is a lead, not a fact.

«The Star», Illinois, 10 de mayo de 1981

What the manuscript says

ANTONIO SALERNO. Services were Friday for Antonio Salerno of Riverdale. Mr. Salerno died Tuesday at Ingalls Memorial hospital, Harvey. The funeral mass was at St. Mary's church, Riverdale. Burial was at St. Benedict's cemetery, Chicago. A former laborer at the Hales and Hunter Feed company in Riverdale, Mr. Salerno was born June 13, 1896, in Italy. He is survived by his wife, Rose; a son, Dominic of Riverdale; a brothr, Vincenzo of Riverdale; two grandchildren, and five great-grandchildren.

Translation

ANTONIO SALERNO. Services for Antonio Salerno, of Riverdale, were held on Friday. Mr Salerno died on Tuesday at Ingalls Memorial Hospital in Harvey. Mass was at St Mary's Church, Riverdale. Burial was at St Benedict Cemetery, Chicago.

A former labourer at the Hales and Hunter Feed Company of Riverdale, Mr Salerno had been born on 13 JUNE 1896 IN ITALY.

He is survived by his wife, Rose; a son, Dominic, of Riverdale; a brother, Vincenzo, of Riverdale; two grandchildren and five great-grandchildren.

What you need to know about this record

  • *** ESTA ESQUELA CIERRA LA IDENTIFICACIÓN. *** Da «13 de junio de 1896», que es EXACTAMENTE la fecha de su acta de nacimiento de Rende. Sus papeles americanos —la naturalización de 1925 y la ficha de reclutamiento de 1942— decían 1897; el periódico acierta donde los formularios fallaban. No hay ninguna duda de que es el hermano de Settimo.
  • MURIÓ UN MARTES: el 10 de mayo de 1981 fue domingo, el funeral fue el viernes 8, así que la muerte cae el MARTES 5 DE MAYO DE 1981. Tenía ochenta y cuatro años.
  • «A BROTHR, VINCENZO OF RIVERDALE». Es la línea más intrigante de todo el documento y está sin resolver — ver el cabo abierto que lleva su nombre.
  • SESENTA Y SIETE AÑOS EN EL MISMO SITIO. Llegó a Riverdale con diecisiete años y allí murió con ochenta y cuatro. La fábrica donde trabajó, la Hales and Hunter, estaba a seis manzanas de su casa.

United States District Court, Northern District of Illinois

Declaración de intención
n.º 22117, agosto de 1921

What the manuscript says

ANTONIO SALERNO. My place of residence is 13832 So. Emerald Ave., Riverdale, Illinois. My occupation is laborer. I was born on the 13th day of June, anno Domini 1897, at Cosenza, Italy. I emigrated to the United States from Naples, Italy, on or about the 8th day of April anno Domini 1914, and arrived at the United States, at the port of New York, on the 16th day of April anno Domini 1914, on the vessel BERLIN. […] My wife's name is Rosina. She was born in Italy and now resides with me. I have 2 children: Domenico born Feb. 11, 1922; Filomena, Feb. 8, 1925, both born at Riverdale, Ill., and reside with me.

Translation

Antonio Salerno, labourer, resident at 13832 South Emerald Avenue, Riverdale, Illinois, declares that he was born on 13 June 1897 at Cosenza, Italy.

HE EMIGRATED FROM NAPLES around 8 April 1914 and ARRIVED AT THE PORT OF NEW YORK ON 16 APRIL 1914, ABOARD THE STEAMER «BERLIN».

He has lived in Illinois since 21 October 1914. His wife is called ROSINA, born in Italy and living with him. He has two children: DOMENICO, born 11 February 1922, and FILOMENA, 8 February 1925, both born at Riverdale.

He renounces all allegiance to Victor Emmanuel III, King of Italy. Signing as witnesses were GABRIELE PRETE, labourer, of 800 South Loomis St., Chicago, and SARFINO ARIELLO, switchman, of 13643 South Halsted St., Riverdale, who declare that they have known him since 1 November 1919.

What you need to know about this record

  • *** AQUÍ ESTÁ EL BARCO. *** Después de agotar el CEMLA sin encontrar a nadie, resulta que la respuesta estaba en el otro hemisferio: Antonio no fue a Buenos Aires, fue a Nueva York, y su nombre está en la lista del vapor BERLIN que atracó el 16 de abril de 1914. La lista de pasajeros de ese viaje es un documento que ahora se puede pedir.
  • SALIÓ EN 1914, NO EN 1919. Toda la reconstrucción familiar daba por hecho que los hermanos emigraron juntos después de la guerra. Antonio se fue CINCO AÑOS ANTES, y con diecisiete recién cumplidos. La familia no se dispersó de golpe: se fue deshilachando.
  • Su año de nacimiento aquí es 1897, y en su acta de Rende es 1896. El día y el mes coinciden exactamente. Su esquela de 1981 dice 1896 y zanja la cuestión.
  • El testigo Gabriele Prete reaparece en el censo de 1950 viviendo a dos casas. Y el otro, Sarfino Ariello, vivía en el 13643 de Halsted, la misma manzana donde el censo sitúa a los Cundari. Era un barrio entero de paisanos.

Second World War draft card

Riverdale, Illinois · serial 798, orden 11239 · 16 de febrero de 1942

Archivo
National Personnel Records Center, St. Louis, Missouri
Vía

n.º 11, Harvey, condado de Cook, Illinois

What the manuscript says

Antonio (None) Salerno. 13838 Emerald Ave., Riverdale, Cook, Illinois. Telephone Riverdale 188 J. Age 44. Date of birth June 13, 1897. Place of birth Cosenza, Italy. Name and address of person who will always know your address: GUS CUNDARI, 13851 S. Halsted, Riverdale, Illinois. Employer: Hales and Hunter, 140th and Halsted. — White, 5'4", 145 lbs, eyes brown, hair black, complexion dark. Other obvious physical characteristics: None.

Translation

Registration card for what was called the «old man's registration», the census of men aged 45 to 64 that the United States took on entering the war. Antonio Salerno, no middle name, of 13838 Emerald Avenue, Riverdale. Telephone: Riverdale 188 J. Forty-four years old, born 13 June 1897 at Cosenza, Italy.

Person who will always know his address: GUS CUNDARI, of 13851 South Halsted, Riverdale. Employed at Hales and Hunter, at 140th and Halsted.

DESCRIPTION: white, FIVE FEET FOUR INCHES (1.63 m), ONE HUNDRED AND FORTY-FIVE POUNDS (66 kg), BROWN EYES, BLACK HAIR, DARK COMPLEXION. No other distinguishing marks.

What you need to know about this record

  • *** ES LA ÚNICA DESCRIPCIÓN FÍSICA DE NADIE EN TODO ESTE ÁRBOL. *** De ciento treinta personas y doscientos años de papeles, éste es el único documento que dice cómo era alguien: bajo, fuerte, moreno de piel, pelo negro y ojos marrones. Su hermano Settimo sería probablemente parecido.
  • GUS CUNDARI COMO CONTACTO. Cundari es el apellido de su suegra, Cherubina Cundari, y también el de Anna Cundari, la mujer con la que se casó su hermano Giovanni en Rende en 1926. Dos hermanos Salerno emparentados con Cundari, uno en Rende y otro en Illinois.
  • En 1942 seguía en la misma fábrica y en la misma calle que en 1925. Sólo había cambiado de número: del 13832 al 13838.

Translation

In April 1950 the house at 13838 Emerald Avenue held three generations:

ANTHONY SALERNO, head of household, 53, married, born in Italy, naturalised. Forty hours a week as a labourer in a feed mill. ROSE, his wife, 43, born in Italy, naturalised. DOMINICK D., the son, 27, married, RAILROAD COMPANY SWITCHMAN, 36 hours. MARY L., his daughter-in-law, 21. BEVERLEE, the granddaughter, 2.

Two houses along, on the same census sheet, lived CONSTANTINO CUNDARI, 62, born in Italy, foreman with the railroad company, with his wife ANGELINA, 57.

What you need to know about this record

  • Su hija Filomena, de veinticinco años en 1950, ya no vive en casa: se habría casado.
  • El hijo entró en el ferrocarril, no en la fábrica de pienso. La generación siguiente salió del trabajo del padre.

What the manuscript says

Salerno Francesco, di anni venticinque, agricoltore…

Translation

In the year eighteen hundred and ninety-six, on the fourteenth day of June, at seven in the morning, at the town hall, before Spina Raffaele, secretary delegated by act of the mayor of the first of December eighteen hundred and ninety-five, there appeared SALERNO FRANCESCO, aged twenty-five, farmer, resident at Rende.

He declares that of CHIAPPETTA FILOMENA, housewife, his wife living with him, there was born on the thirteenth of the current month, in the house situated in contrada S. Agostino, a boy, upon whom he bestows the name ANTONIO.

Witnesses: Greco Pasquale, farmer; and Serpe Serafino, farmer.

What you need to know about this record

  • Es el acta que permitió detectar el desfase del catálogo de Antenati. El año manuscrito dice «novantasei» con toda claridad, y la edad del padre (25) cuadra exactamente con su nacimiento en diciembre de 1870.
  • La familia se ha mudado de contrada Saporito a contrada S. Agostino, donde seguirá en 1900.
Sibling

Umberto Salerno

Third son, born at Rende in 1897. He was 22 in 1919.

Child of Francesco Salerno · Filomena Chiappetta

Registered the same day

He was born on 22 November 1897 at six in the morning, in the contrada Saporito. By ten past ten that same morning his father was at the town hall declaring him: four hours and ten minutes between the birth and the registration.

The age Francesco declared is written in very faint ink; the likeliest reading is twenty-four, and he was twenty-six. The witnesses were two village farmers, Spizzirri Giovanni, forty-nine, and Picarelli Vincenzo, sixty-eight. Nobody signed but the secretary, Spina Raffaele.

His record was harder to find than any other. The 1897 register has re-photographed pages: when the volume has a loose sheet lying on it — death annotations that other town halls would send decades later — the digitiser takes two shots of the same opening. Page 86 carries a sheet covering half the spread; page 85 is the clean one, and that is the one that was saved.

With Umberto the family had gone back to the contrada Saporito, where they had already been in 1895. In 1919 he was twenty-two and was one of those who left.

What the manuscript says

Salerno Francesco, contadino… di anni ventiquattro…

Translation

In the year eighteen hundred and ninety-seven, on the twenty-second day of November, at ten past ten in the morning, at the town hall, before Spina Raffaele, secretary delegated by act of the mayor of the sixteenth of December eighteen hundred and ninety-six, duly approved, there appeared SALERNO FRANCESCO, farmhand, resident at Rende.

He declares that of CHIAPPETTA FILOMENA, countrywoman, his wife living with him, there was born on the twenty-second of the current month, at six in the morning, in the house situated in contrada Saporito, a child of the male sex, upon whom he bestows the name UMBERTO.

Witnesses: Spizzirri Giovanni, aged forty-nine, farmer; and Picarelli Vincenzo, aged sixty-eight, farmer, both resident in the comune. Illiterate: they do not sign; only the registrar signs.

What you need to know about this record

  • La edad declarada del padre está escrita con tinta muy pálida. La lectura más probable es «ventiquattro» (24), pero Francesco tenía 26 en noviembre de 1897. Otra edad declarada errónea.
  • La familia ha vuelto a contrada Saporito, donde ya estaba en 1895. En 1896 estaba en S. Agostino y en 1899 estará en Campagnano. Se mudaban mucho.
  • Para localizar esta acta hubo que descubrir que el digitalizador refotografía la misma apertura dos veces cuando lleva un papel suelto encima. Aquí se ha guardado la toma limpia.
Direct line

Settimo Salerno

In the records: Settimo — no «Settino»

The great-grandfather Gonzalo lived to know. A twin, the first born, at half past four in the morning of 24 October 1899 at Rende. He crossed the Atlantic, married at Santa Fe at twenty-three, and had to fight the Argentine civil registry for twenty-seven years to get his own name spelled correctly.

Child of Francesco Salerno · Filomena Chiappetta

Married Cristina Rizzo

Parent of Carlos Francisco Salerno · Nilda Salerno

Twenty-five minutes the elder

He was born on 24 October 1899 at half past four in the morning, in the contrada Campagnano at Rende. His brother Saverio was born twenty-five minutes later. Four days after, on 28 October at twenty to eleven in the morning, their father registered them in two consecutive records, with the same two witnesses and five minutes between one and the other.

That they were twins is not an inference from the closeness of the numbers: both records say so in writing and refer to each other. His says «e ch'è gemello un altro mi conserva l'atto seguente»; Saverio's, «l'atto precedente». And his adds a line nobody was obliged to write: «Il dichiarante ha inoltre attestato che il bambino suddetto è IL PRIMO NATO». The order of birth was supplied by the father himself, not by a midwife.

The index this investigation started from calls him «Settino». The original record says SETTIMO, quite clearly, both in the body and in the marginal heading. Settimo is the ordinary Italian name for a seventh child; on paper he is the fourth documented. Either the name is only a name, or there were earlier children who are not recorded. It proves nothing, but it points towards the fourteen children of family memory.

He belongs to the class of '99, the ragazzi del '99, the cohort Italy called up in 1917 at barely eighteen; the family keeps his First World War medals. He ended up at Santa Fe, Argentina, where he went by Setimio. His wife, Cristina Rizzo, died there in November 1967, and he between 1977 and 1980, aged seventy-eight or eighty-one. He does not appear in the index of arrivals at the port of Buenos Aires under any spelling of his name.

Marriage

Marriage record with Cristina Rizzo

ciudad de Santa Fe, Argentina · acta 140 · 26 de marzo de 1923

Archivo
Registro Civil de la Provincia de Santa Fe — Archivo General
Cómo se obtuvo
trámite digital en santafe.gob.ar/rcdigital, pedido por Gonzalo el 25 de agosto de 2026
Firma digital
César Gabriel Garrone, Archivo Central Santa Fe, 28-8-2026
Verificación
trámite 26-00-284-316 · hash D436-F555-6EF1-EE25-20B7-FA23-3C5D-8A13-78B9-EA89

Translation

Record number one hundred and forty. In the city of SANTA FE, department of La Capital, province of Santa Fe, on the twenty-sixth day of March in the year nineteen hundred and twenty-three. Before me, head of the Registry of Civil Status, there appeared:

Don SETTIMO SALERNO, aged twenty-three, of ITALIAN nationality, BORN AT RENDE, resident in this city, by trade a BAKER; son of don FRANCISCO SALERNO, aged forty, Italian, RESIDENT AT RENDE, ITALY, farmer, and of doña FILOMENA CHIAPPETTA, aged fifty, Italian, resident at Rende;

and doña CRISTINA RIZZO, aged twenty-three, of ARGENTINE nationality; daughter of don SALVADOR RIZZO and of doña ÁNGELA COSTABILE, Italian, resident in this city.

Both parties stated that it was their will to marry, taking each other as husband and wife... I declare that don SETTIMO SALERNO and doña CRISTINA RIZZO are joined in lawful marriage.

What you need to know about this record

  • *** CONFIRMA A SUS PADRES POR VÍA ARGENTINA. *** El acta italiana de 1899 daba «Salerno Francesco, de 28 años, y Chiappetta Filomena». Ésta, veinticuatro años después y en otro continente, da los mismos dos nombres — y añade que en 1923 SEGUÍAN VIVIENDO EN RENDE. No emigraron con el hijo.
  • *** Y DA POR FIN LOS PADRES DE LA NOVIA: SALVADOR RIZZO y ÁNGELA COSTABILE. *** Hasta este día los Rizzo eran una sola persona suelta en todo el árbol.
  • ERA PANADERO. A los veintitrés años, recién llegado de Rende.
  • OJO CON LA EDAD DEL PADRE: el acta le pone cuarenta años, pero el acta de nacimiento de 1899 le daba veintiocho, o sea que en 1923 tendría cincuenta y dos. Doce años de error — normal, porque el padre estaba en Italia y la edad la dio el hijo de memoria.
  • *** UNA NOTA AL MARGEN DE 1950 RECTIFICA SU PROPIO NOMBRE. *** La Dirección General del Registro Civil hizo constar, veintisiete años después, «que el verdadero nombre del contrayente es SETTIMO». En su acta de boda estaba mal escrito.
Fotografía

The four brothers in Argentina

Argentina — estudio fotográfico · fotografía de familia · hacia 1925-1930

Procedencia
álbum familiar, rama argentina
Soporte
copia de estudio, telón pintado con palmera

Translation

Four young men posing in a photographic studio, in front of a painted backdrop with a palm tree and a landscape. Two standing, in dark suits; two seated on a bench, in light summer suits with their hats on their knees — a straw boater with a dark band on the left, a soft-brimmed felt on the right. All in white stiff-collared shirts and wide dark ties.

The family identifies them as the FOUR SALERNO BROTHERS newly arrived in Argentina. SETTIMO and SAVERIO are certainly among them; about the other two there is no certainty.

What you need to know about this record

  • *** LA FOTO CORRIGE LA FECHA DE LA EMIGRACIÓN. *** La familia siempre dijo que llegaron «en 1919, después de la guerra». La ropa dice otra cosa: el canotier de paja y los pantalones de raya ancha con cintura alta son de mediados de los años veinte para adelante. Y ninguno de los cuatro aparenta menos de veinte largos: si ARTURO, nacido en 1907, es uno de ellos, en 1919 tendría DOCE AÑOS, y ahí no hay ningún chico de doce. Conclusión: o Arturo no está en la foto, o LLEGARON HACIA 1925-1930, no en 1919. Y lo segundo encaja con todo lo demás: Antonio se fue solo en 1914, Giovanni seguía en Rende casándose en 1926. La familia no se rompió de golpe en 1919 — se fue deshilachando a lo largo de quince años. La fecha de 1919 es memoria, y la memoria ya falló una vez con Antonio.
  • *** LOS DOS SENTADOS PODRÍAN SER LOS MELLIZOS. *** Es una hipótesis de mirada, no un dato: son los dos que visten igual —traje claro de verano—, están sentados en posición simétrica, los dos con el sombrero sobre la rodilla, y tienen la cara más parecida entre sí que ninguno de los otros dos pares. Settimo y Saverio nacieron el mismo día de 1899 con veinticinco minutos de diferencia. Nadie que lo sepa puede mirar esa foto sin pensarlo.
  • QUIÉNES PUEDEN SER LOS CUATRO, por eliminación documental. De los siete hijos varones, ANTONIO se fue a Chicago en 1914 y allí murió en 1981; GIOVANNI se quedó en Rende y se casó allí en 1926. Quedan cinco candidatos para cuatro sillas: GIUSEPPE (1895), UMBERTO (1897), SETTIMO (1899), SAVERIO (1899) y ARTURO (1907).
  • Y AQUÍ HAY ALGO QUE MERECE PENSARSE. La familia recuerda a los que vinieron como «Saverio, Settimo, y creería que Raffaele y Angelo». Pero Raffaele y Angelo no aparecen en ninguna partida, y en cambio UMBERTO y ARTURO sí existen y su destino está completamente en blanco. En esta genealogía ya ha pasado dos veces que una persona viviera con un nombre distinto del registrado: SETTIMO se hacía llamar Setimio en Argentina, y AQUILA figura como Rosalia en su propia acta de matrimonio. No sería extraño que los hombres que la familia recuerda como Raffaele y Angelo sean Umberto y Arturo.
  • Un dato para no confundirse: ANGELINA, la hermana nacida en 1902, MURIÓ EN RENDE EL 29 DE AGOSTO DE 1910, con ocho años. No pudo emigrar. Si la memoria familiar recuerda un «Angelo», o es otra persona o es ella mal recordada.
  • Lo que la foto sí prueba, sin discusión: LLEGARON JUNTOS Y SE HICIERON UN RETRATO. Cuatro hermanos de un pueblo de Calabria, con el traje bueno y el sombrero en la mano, posando delante de una palmera pintada el día que empezaron otra vida. Es la única imagen de esta familia anterior a la generación de los abuelos.

What the manuscript says

…a cui dà il nome di Settimo, e ch'è gemello un altro mi conserva l'atto seguente.

Il dichiarante ha inoltre attestato che il bambino suddetto è il primo nato.

Translation

In the year eighteen hundred and ninety-nine, on the twenty-eighth day of October, at twenty to eleven in the morning, at the town hall, before Pisani Domenico, senior assessor for the mayor being indisposed, there appeared SALERNO FRANCESCO, aged twenty-eight, farmhand, resident at Rende.

He declares that of CHIAPPETTA FILOMENA, countrywoman, his wife living with him, there was born on the twenty-fourth of the current month, at half past four in the morning, in the house situated in contrada Campagnano, a child of the male sex, to whom he gives the name SETTIMO, and who is the twin of another recorded in the following entry.

Witnesses: Perri Pietro, aged sixty-one, farmhand; and Spizzirri Giovanni, aged forty-six, farmhand, both resident in the comune.

The informant further attested that the said child is THE FIRST BORN.

What you need to know about this record

  • EL NOMBRE NO ES «SETTINO» SINO SETTIMO. El índice del que se partía lo transcribe mal. El acta original lo dice con claridad tanto en el cuerpo como en el epígrafe del margen.
  • La edad del padre (28) es CORRECTA, nacido en diciembre de 1870. Es de las pocas veces que Francesco declara bien su edad.
  • «Settimo» significa séptimo, pero por el índice es el cuarto hijo documentado. O el nombre es sólo un nombre, o hubo hermanos antes de 1899 que no constan. No es prueba de nada, pero apunta hacia los catorce hijos de la memoria familiar.
Sibling

Saverio Salerno

Settimo's twin, the second born, twenty-five minutes later. He was 20 in 1919.

Child of Francesco Salerno · Filomena Chiappetta

The second, by twenty-five minutes

He was born at four fifty-five in the morning on 24 October 1899, in the same house in the contrada Campagnano and twenty-five minutes after his brother.

His record was drawn up at ten forty-five on 28 October, five minutes after Settimo's, before the same official and with the same two witnesses — Perri Pietro, sixty-one, and Spizzirri Giovanni, forty-six, both farmhands. It closes with the mirror of the other's formula: «il bambino soddetto è IL SECONDO NATO». None of those present signed.

In 1919 he was twenty and was one of those who left. He does not appear in the Buenos Aires arrivals index, just like his twin.

What the manuscript says

…a cui dà il nome di Saverio, e ch'è gemello un altro mi conserva l'atto precedente.

Il dichiarante ha inoltre attestato che il bambino soddetto è il secondo nato.

Translation

In the year eighteen hundred and ninety-nine, on the twenty-eighth day of October, at a quarter to eleven in the morning — five minutes after the preceding record — at the town hall, before Pisani Domenico, there appeared SALERNO FRANCESCO, aged twenty-eight, farmhand.

He declares that of CHIAPPETTA FILOMENA, countrywoman, his wife, there was born on the twenty-fourth of the current month, at four fifty-five in the morning, in the house situated in contrada Campagnano, a child of the male sex, to whom he gives the name SAVERIO, and who is the twin of another recorded in the preceding entry.

Witnesses: the same two as in the preceding record.

The informant further attested that the said child is THE SECOND BORN.

What you need to know about this record

  • Los gemelos no son una deducción por la proximidad de los números: las dos actas lo dicen por escrito y se remiten la una a la otra.
  • El dato del parto doble lo aporta el propio padre («il dichiarante ha inoltre attestato»), no la comadrona.
Sibling

Angelina Salerno

Born in contrada Campagnano on 14 May 1902 and dead in contrada Sant'Agostino on 29 August 1910, aged eight years and three months. She did not emigrate.

Child of Francesco Salerno · Filomena Chiappetta

The record made her ten

She was born on 14 May 1902 at half past twelve, in the contrada Campagnano. Her father registered her two days later and declared thirty years: he was thirty-one. The witnesses were Presta Francesco, a twenty-six-year-old day labourer, and Bartucci Antonio, a seventy-year-old farmhand.

She died on 29 August 1910 at noon, in the contrada Sant'Agostino, in the same house where her brothers Giovanni and Arturo and her sister Maria had been born. The death record says «di anni dieci», ten years old. It is wrong, and the proof is two registers away: her own birth record. She was eight years and three months old.

It was not her parents who reported the death. It was her uncle Salerno Domenico, fifty, and her maternal grandfather, Chiappetta Giovanni, sixty, and between them they rounded the child's age badly. The grandfather signed the record; the others declared themselves illiterate. The witnesses were a twenty-six-year-old farmhand and a thirty-five-year-old barber.

The margin of her birth record is blank: the death was never noted there, although the record exists and sits in the next register along. For a time it was assumed that Angelina had emigrated to Argentina with the family. She did not: she died nine years before her brothers sailed.

è comparso Salerno Francesco, di anni trenta, bracciante, domiciliato in Rende…

contadina seco lui convivente… il nome di Angelina

Translation

In the year nineteen hundred and two, on the sixteenth day of May, at five past ten in the morning, at the town hall, before Pisani Domenico, assessor for the mayor being indisposed, Registrar of the Civil Status of the comune of Rende, there appeared SALERNO FRANCESCO, aged thirty, BRACCIANTE (day labourer), resident at Rende.

He declares that at half past twelve on the fourteenth of the current month, in the house situated in CONTRADA CAMPAGNANO, of CHIAPPETTA FILOMENA, his wife, countrywoman, living with him, there was born a child of the female sex whom he presents to me, and to whom he gives the name ANGELINA.

Witnesses: Presta Francesco, aged twenty-six, day labourer; and Bartucci Antonio, aged seventy, farmhand, both resident in this comune.

The record having been read to those present, they did not sign it with me, being illiterate, as they declared. Only Domenico Pisani signs.

What you need to know about this record

  • El margen del acta 55 está EN BLANCO: la muerte de Angelina en 1910 no se anotó nunca al margen de su nacimiento, aunque el acta de defunción existe y está aquí al lado.
  • El oficio del padre baila con los años: «contadino» en 1899, «bracciante» aquí en 1902, y otra vez «contadino» en 1904, 1907 y 1909.
  • La contrada también cambia: Campagnano en 1899 y 1902, Santo Agostino a partir de 1904.

Translation

In the year nineteen hundred and ten, on the thirtieth day of August, at thirteen hours, at the town hall, before VERCILLO GIUSEPPE, assessor for the mayor, Registrar of the Civil Status of the comune of Rende, there appeared SALERNO DOMENICO, aged fifty, farmhand, resident at Rende, and CHIAPPETTA GIOVANNI, aged sixty, farmhand, resident at Rende.

Who declared to me that at twelve o'clock YESTERDAY, in the house situated in CONTRADA SANT'AGOSTINO, there died SALERNO ANGELINA, aged ten, countrywoman, resident at Rende, born at Rende, daughter of FRANCESCO, farmhand, resident at Rende, and of CHIAPPETTA FILOMENA, countrywoman, resident at Rende.

Witnesses: Perri Domenico, aged twenty-six, farmhand; and Vercillo Ferdinando, aged thirty-five, BARBER, both resident in this comune.

The record having been read to all present, I signed it with the informant CHIAPPETTA and with the witness VERCILLO, the others having stated that they were illiterate.

What you need to know about this record

  • LA EDAD DEL ACTA ESTÁ MAL. Dice «di anni dieci», diez años. Angelina nació el 14 de mayo de 1902 —su acta de nacimiento está aquí mismo—, así que el 29 de agosto de 1910 tenía OCHO AÑOS Y TRES MESES. Quienes declaran no son los padres, sino un tío y un abuelo, y redondean de más.
  • LOS DECLARANTES SON EL DATO BUENO. El primero, SALERNO DOMENICO de cincuenta años, es el hermano mayor de Francesco: diez semanas más tarde se casaba con Giuseppina Cairo declarando cincuenta y uno. Las dos cifras se cruzan en un nacimiento de hacia 1859.
  • El segundo, CHIAPPETTA GIOVANNI de sesenta años, es el padre de Filomena, el abuelo materno. Ya constaba como padre de ella en el acta de nacimiento de 1877. Seguía vivo, y SABÍA FIRMAR: es de los pocos de la familia que no firma con una cruz.
  • No comparece ninguno de los dos padres de la niña.
Sibling

Giovanni Salerno

Born on the first day of 1904, four years after the death of the uncle whose name he carried. He was 15 in 1919 — and in 1926 he married at Rende.

Child of Francesco Salerno · Filomena Chiappetta

He stayed at Rende

He was born on 1 January 1904 at two in the morning, in the contrada Santo Agostino: the family's first birth of the year, and one of the register's first records, number 5. His father registered him on the 3rd at ten in the morning and declared thirty-four years. He carries the name of his uncle, dead four years earlier at twenty-six.

In the margin of the record a later hand wrote, with a flourish: «Addì 24-7-1926 in Rende n° 34 sposò Cundari Anna». He married at Rende on 24 July 1926. That line changed the story: if he was in the village in 1926, either he never sailed or he came back. He was fifteen when the older ones left.

It is the document that best supports what the family tells: that the household split in two, that the elder brothers crossed, and that Francesco and Filomena stayed at Rende with the younger ones. And it means there is a living branch there, descended from Giovanni and Anna Cundari.

What the manuscript says

Translation

In the year nineteen hundred and four, on the third day of January, at ten in the morning, at the town hall, before MERCAVALLO MICHELANGELO, secretary delegated by act of the mayor dated the twentieth of August nineteen hundred and two, approved, Registrar of the Civil Status of the comune of Rende, there appeared SALERNO FRANCESCO, aged thirty-four, farmhand, resident at Rende.

He declares that at two in the morning on the FIRST of the current month, in the house situated in CONTRADA SANTO AGOSTINO, of CHIAPPETTA FILOMENA, his wife, countrywoman, living with him, there was born a child of the male sex whom he presents to me, and to whom he gives the name GIOVANNI.

Witnesses: Cosentino Rosario, aged thirty-four, farmhand; and Alfano Domenico, aged forty-five, farmhand, both resident in this comune.

The record having been read to those present, they did not sign it with me, being illiterate, as they declared. Only M. Mercavallo signs.

What you need to know about this record

  • ANOTACIÓN MARGINAL, de mano posterior y con rúbrica: «Addì 24-7-1926 in Rende n° 34 sposò Cundari Anna». Giovanni se casó en Rende el 24 de julio de 1926, acta nº 34, con CUNDARI ANNA. Dato nuevo.
  • Y dato de peso: si se casó en Rende en 1926, o no embarcó con la familia en 1919 o volvió. Conviene revisar el supuesto de que emigraron todos.
  • Nació el 1 de enero: es de los primeros nacimientos del año, y por eso su acta lleva el número 5 y está casi al principio del volumen — el índice de este registro es cortísimo.
  • La familia ya vive en contrada Santo Agostino; en 1902, con Angelina, estaban en Campagnano.
Sibling

Arturo Salerno

Born before dawn on 21 December 1907 and registered that same morning. He was 12 in 1919.

Child of Francesco Salerno · Filomena Chiappetta

A carpenter and a shoemaker

He was born on 21 December 1907 at half past four in the morning, in the contrada S. Agostino, and his father registered him that same morning at ten to twelve: seven hours after the birth.

The witnesses on his record are not the usual day labourers. Signing as witnesses are Sicilia Pasquale, a sixty-one-year-old carpenter, and Bruno Giovanni, a seventy-seven-year-old shoemaker — two village trades and two old men. Francesco declared thirty-eight years on the very day he turned thirty-seven.

His record sits in a treacherous volume: Rende's «ordinary» register 99 has only two pages, and the real one is «supplement 2», with ninety-four. It is closed by Domenico Pisani at Rende on 1 January 1908.

He was twelve in 1919. He stayed in Italy with his parents.

Translation

In the year nineteen hundred and seven, on the twenty-first day of December, at ten to twelve in the morning, at the town hall, before PISANI DOMENICO, assessor for the mayor, Registrar of the Civil Status of the comune of Rende, there appeared SALERNO FRANCESCO, aged thirty-eight, farmhand, resident at Rende.

He declares that at half past four in the morning on the twenty-first of the current month, in the house situated in CONTRADA S. AGOSTINO, of CHIAPPETTA FILOMENA, countrywoman, his wife, living with him, there was born a child of the male sex whom he presents to me, and to whom he gives the name ARTURO.

Witnesses: Sicilia Pasquale, aged sixty-one, CARPENTER; and Bruno Giovanni, aged seventy-seven, SHOEMAKER, both resident in this comune.

The record having been read to those present, they did not sign it with me, being illiterate, as they declared. Only Domenico Pisani signs.

What you need to know about this record

  • Nació y se inscribió EL MISMO DÍA: parto a las 4:30 de la madrugada, acta a las 11:50 de la mañana, siete horas después. Es el registro más rápido de toda la familia.
  • Los dos testigos no son campesinos, como suele ocurrir, sino artesanos del pueblo: un carpintero de 61 años y un zapatero de 77.
  • El volumen es el Reg. 99 «suppl. 2». El Reg. 99 «normal» tiene sólo dos páginas: es un resto. El suplemento se cierra con la firma de Domenico Pisani el 1 de enero de 1908.
Sibling

Maria Salerno

The youngest of the nine documented children. Born in contrada S. Agostino on the night of 23 September 1909; she was 10 in 1919.

Child of Francesco Salerno · Filomena Chiappetta

The clerk forgot his own name

She was born on the night of 23 September 1909, at twenty-five to twelve, in the contrada S. Agostino. She is the youngest of the nine documented children. Her father registered her the next morning and this time declared his age correctly: thirty-eight.

The record has an odd gap. The line «Avanti di me» — before me — was left blank: the clerk never got round to writing his own name. The man signing at the foot is Federico Costabile, who on neighbouring records appears as assessor delegated for civil registration. The witnesses were two farmhands, Ruffolo Serafino, fifty-three, and Rende Tommaso, thirty-five.

On the same opening of the register, the next record carries as a witness a «Salerno Francesco, di anni trentotto, contadino»: same age, same trade. It is him, staying on as a witness for the neighbour after registering his daughter. That record was reported by a midwife because the husband «è in America». The village was already emigrating.

She was ten in 1919 and stayed with her parents. The family remembers a «Marietta» who ended up in Canada; Marietta is the ordinary diminutive of Maria, so it could be her or her aunt Maria Chiappetta, born in 1901. No document settles it.

What the manuscript says

Translation

In the year nineteen hundred and nine, on the twenty-fourth day of September, at twenty to eleven in the morning, at the town hall. Before [the line for the registrar's name was left BLANK], Registrar of the Civil Status of the comune of Rende, there appeared SALERNO FRANCESCO, aged thirty-eight, farmhand, resident at Rende.

He declares that at twenty-three thirty-five on the twenty-third of the current month, in the house situated in CONTRADA S. AGOSTINO, of CHIAPPETTA FILOMENA, countrywoman, his LAWFUL wife, living with him, there was born a child of the female sex whom he presents to me, and to whom he gives the name MARIA.

Witnesses: Ruffolo Serafino, aged fifty-three, farmhand; and Rende Tommaso, aged thirty-five, farmhand, both resident in this comune.

The record having been read to all present, they did not sign it with me, being illiterate. Signed FEDERICO COSTABILE.

What you need to know about this record

  • El escribano se dejó SIN RELLENAR la línea «Avanti di me»: el acta no dice quién la autorizó. Lo resuelve la firma del pie, Federico Costabile, que en las actas vecinas de la misma apertura consta como «assessore delegato per gli atti di Stato Civile».
  • Aquí la edad declarada del padre es EXACTA por una vez: treinta y ocho años, y había nacido en diciembre de 1870. En 1902 y 1904 se había quedado corto o largo por un año.
  • En la MISMA apertura, el acta 207 lleva como testigo a un «Salerno Francesco, di anni trentotto, contadino»: misma edad y mismo oficio. Es él, que se quedó de testigo del vecino después de inscribir a su hija. Y ese acta 207 lo declara una comadrona porque el marido «è in America»: el pueblo ya estaba emigrando.
Nephew/niece

Salvatore Salerno

A first cousin, not a brother. Son of Giovanni and Vincenzina. He was thirteen months old when his father died.

Child of Giovanni Salerno · Vincenzina Morelli

He was taken for a brother

He was born on 12 January 1899 at three in the morning, in a contrada written in a hand so fine that it reads «Mari», «Nari» or «Maio» and is none of the ones known to the family. His father registered him the following day at twenty past nine, before Pisani Domenico. The witnesses were a fifty-one-year-old farmer and a thirty-year-old farmhand.

His record was ordered on the assumption that he was another child of Francesco and Filomena. He is not, and the original is emphatic: the informant is SALERNO GIOVANNI and the mother MORELLI VINCENZINA. Salvatore is the son of Francesco's younger brother — that is, Settimo's first cousin and not his brother. It fits without forcing anything: his parents married in October 1897 and he was born in January 1899.

The find also produced the hardest fact yet about his father. When Giovanni died, on 23 February 1900, Salvatore was thirteen months old. His mother was left a widow with him. And one more age that does not add up: Giovanni declared twenty-four years when registering him, when he had turned twenty-six.

In the margin of the record a twentieth-century hand noted: «Addì 18.10.1921 in Rende n° 67 sposò Misuraca Caterina Santa». He married at Rende in October 1921, aged twenty-two.

Translation

In the year eighteen hundred and ninety-nine, on the thirteenth day of January, at twenty past nine in the morning, at the town hall, before Pisani Domenico, senior assessor for the mayor being indisposed, there appeared SALERNO GIOVANNI, aged twenty-four, farmhand, resident at Rende.

He declares that of MORELLI VINCENZINA, countrywoman, his wife living with him, there was born on the twelfth of the current month, at three in the morning, in the house situated in contrada Mari, a child of the male sex, upon whom he bestows the name SALVATORE.

What you need to know about this record

  • ESTE SALVATORE ES SOBRINO, NO HERMANO. Se pidió el acta creyendo que era hijo de Francesco y Filomena, y el original es tajante: el declarante es Salerno GIOVANNI y la madre MORELLI VINCENZINA.
  • Encaja perfectamente: boda de sus padres en octubre de 1897, primer hijo en enero de 1899. Y da un dato duro: cuando Giovanni murió el 23 de febrero de 1900, dejaba un hijo de trece meses.
  • Anotación marginal de mano del siglo XX: «Addì 18.10.1921 in Rende n° 67 sposò Misuraca Caterina Santa».
  • Si la familia esperaba un hermano llamado Salvatore, hay que buscarlo en otro año.

Marriage

Marriage record

Rende · acta 44 · 5 de noviembre de 1910, 9:30 h

Fondo
Stato civile italiano > Rende > Registro 102 «Matrimoni» 1910
Registro
an_ua35988769 · hash wbVx1jZ
Página
24 de 34
Año manuscrito
«millenovecento dieci» — coincide con el catálogo
Registrar
Pisani Domenico, asesor delegado por el alcalde

What the manuscript says

1.° Salerno Domenico, vedovo, di anni cinquantuno, contadino, nato in Castrolibero,
residente in Rende, figlio di Michele, residente in Rende, e di Palermo Maria,
residente in Rende;
2.° Cairo Giuseppina, nubile, di anni trentadue, contadina, nata in Rende,
residente in Rende, figlia di fu Giovanni, residente in vita in Rende,
e di fu Busatta Rosa, residente in vita in Rende

Translation

In the year nineteen hundred and ten, on the fifth day of November, at half past nine in the morning, at the town hall of Rende, open to the public. Before PISANI DOMENICO, assessor delegated by the mayor, Registrar of the Civil Status, in official dress, there appeared in person:

1st SALERNO DOMENICO, WIDOWER, aged FIFTY-ONE, farmhand, BORN AT CASTROLIBERO, resident at Rende, son of MICHELE, resident at Rende, and of PALERMO MARIA, resident at Rende;

2nd CAIRO GIUSEPPINA, unmarried, aged THIRTY-TWO, countrywoman, born at Rende, resident at Rende, daughter of the late GIOVANNI, who in life resided at Rende, and of the late ROSA BUSATTA, who in life resided at Rende.

Who requested me to unite them in marriage. To that end they presented me with one document, described below; and from the examination of that, and of those already produced when the banns were requested — all of them, with my endorsement, incorporated into the volume of annexes of this register — it appearing to me that nothing stands in the way of the celebration of their marriage, I read to the spouses articles one hundred and thirty, one hundred and thirty-one and one hundred and thirty-two of the Civil Code, and then asked the husband whether he intends to take as his wife the CAIRO GIUSEPPINA here present, and her whether she intends to take as her husband the SALERNO DOMENICO here present; and each having answered me in the affirmative, in the full hearing also of the witnesses named below, I pronounced in the name of the law that they are united in marriage.

Present at this act were: LO CELSO RAFFAELE, aged forty-eight, TAILOR, and SOTTILE GASPARE, aged twenty-seven, GENTLEMAN, both resident in this comune.

The only document presented to me is the certificate of the banns celebrated by me on the successive Sundays the sixteenth and the twenty-third of October of the current year.

The record having been read to those present, I signed it only with the witnesses, the spouses having stated that they were illiterate. Signed by Lo Celso Raffaele, Sottile Gaspare and Domenico Pisani. THE COUPLE DO NOT SIGN.

What you need to know about this record

  • LA EDAD DE DOMENICO, POR FIN. Cincuenta y un años el 5 de noviembre de 1910: nació entre noviembre de 1858 y noviembre de 1859. Y diez semanas antes, declarando la muerte de su sobrina Angelina, había dicho cincuenta. Las dos cifras se cruzan en 1859. Encaja con la boda de sus padres, celebrada en Rende el 22 de agosto de 1858: Domenico sería el primogénito, nacido unos diez u once meses después.
  • EL PUEBLO: CASTROLIBERO. El acta lo dice sin ambigüedad. Choca con el barrido que daba Castrolibero por descartado tras leer los catorce años de 1859 a 1872. La explicación más probable es que el barrido empezara en 1859 y él naciera a finales de 1858 o en los primeros meses de 1859 — o que su acta esté inscrita como DE RANGO, el apellido que Michele usaba en Castrolibero, y se pasara por alto al buscar «Salerno». Hay que volver a Castrolibero, Nati 1858 y 1859, buscando DE RANGO DOMENICO de Michele y Maria Palermo.
  • VIUDO. Queda confirmado que no era su primera boda. El acta no dice de quién enviudó ni cuándo, así que siguen pendientes ese primer matrimonio y la defunción de la primera mujer.
  • MICHELE Y MARIA ANTONIA SEGUÍAN VIVOS. Es el hallazgo colateral y es de peso. El formulario distingue vivos de muertos con rigor: de la novia dice «figlia di FU Giovanni, residente IN VITA in Rende», y en el acta 43 de la misma página, «figlio di FU Francesco». Del novio, en cambio, dice «figlio di Michele, residente in Rende, e di Palermo Maria, residente in Rende», sin «fu» y sin «in vita». Michele Salerno, el expósito de 1825, y Maria Antonia Palermo estaban vivos en Rende el 5 de noviembre de 1910, con 85 y 71 años.
  • El apellido de la madre de la novia está en el límite de resolución del escaneo: se lee «Busatta», pero podría ser «Bosatta» o «Barsatta». El original mide 3008 px y no da más.
  • No consta consentimiento de padres, coherente con las edades de los novios.
Ancestor

Domenico Faienza

In the records: Domingo Faienza en Argentina

Son of Nicola Faienza and Angiolina Pizzi. He emigrated to Argentina, where he died at Santa Fe on 13 December 1985, aged eighty-five. *** HIS ARGENTINE DEATH CERTIFICATE TURNED UP IN AUGUST 2026 AND CHANGED EVERYTHING: *** it says he was BORN AT PAGLIETA (Italy) on 1 March 1900, and it confirms his parents. The Italian record has still not been found, but now it is known exactly where and when to look — and why it never surfaced: it had been sought in 1899, and he was born in 1900.

ATESSA en 1899. Índice anual completo leído: 396 nacimientos y cuatro Faienza — Antonio, Carmine, Luzio y Rosina—, ningún Domenico. Y los cuatro se han abierto: son de otras tres familias Faienza del pueblo, no de la nuestra
De qué pueblo son los FAIENZA
ATESSA: el acta de matrimonio de 1892 declara al padre nacido y residente allí, y al abuelo paterno también
Dónde nació ÉL
PAGLIETA — y no es lo mismo. Los padres se casaron en Atessa en diciembre de 1892 y se mudaron a Paglieta, a trece kilómetros, donde nació el hijo ocho años después
Dónde hay que buscarlo
*** PAGLIETA, NACIMIENTOS DE 1900, un varón nacido el 1 de marzo. *** Pero NO por internet: Antenati sólo tiene Paglieta hasta 1899. Hay que pedírsela al AYUNTAMIENTO DE PAGLIETA, que es quien guarda los libros que el Archivio di Stato todavía no ha recibido. Translation

At SANTA FE, department of La Capital, province of Santa Fe, Argentine Republic, on 19 December 1985. I, Public Officer of this Registry, record the DEATH of DOMINGO FAIENZA. Male, ITALIAN nationality. Status: married. Occupation: RETIRED. Identity document: Cédula 177,029. Address: J. P. López 2643.

SON OF NICOLÁS FAIENZA AND ÁNGELA PIZZI.

BORN AT PAGLIETA (ITALY) ON 1 MARCH 1900.

Occurring at Santa Fe on 13 December 1985 at 19:30. Cause of death: illness. Medical certificate of Doctor César Carli, 19 December 1985. Informant: SIXTO GÓMEZ, L.E. 5,261,009, address Berutti 5663, acting by virtue of being a person authorised for that purpose. — Arsenia del R. Ibáñez de Quintana, Deputy Head of the Deaths Subsection.

What you need to know about this record

  • *** DEVUELVE PAGLIETA AL MAPA, Y CORRIGE ALGO QUE YO HABÍA DADO POR CERRADO. *** Tras leer el acta de matrimonio de Atessa de 1892 escribí que la pista de Paglieta quedaba tumbada «definitivamente». Estaba mal. Aquel acta probaba que NICOLA, el padre, había nacido y vivía en Atessa en 1892 — no decía nada de dónde nacería su hijo ocho años después. Esta partida lo dice en letra clara: PAGLIETA. Las dos cosas son compatibles.
  • *** Y EXPLICA POR QUÉ NO LO ENCONTRABA: BUSQUÉ EN EL AÑO EQUIVOCADO. *** Se barrieron enteros los índices de nacimientos de Paglieta 1899 y de Atessa 1899 sin hallar ningún Domenico Faienza. Normal: nació el 1 de marzo de **1900**, un año que no se ha mirado nunca. Aquellos negativos siguen valiendo, pero eran del año que no tocaba.
  • CONFIRMA A SUS PADRES POR VÍA ARGENTINA. «Hijo de NICOLÁS FAIENZA y de ÁNGELA PIZZI» es exactamente la pareja del acta de matrimonio de Atessa de 1892. Dos archivos de dos países distintos, sin relación entre sí, dan los mismos dos nombres.
  • MURIÓ SIENDO ITALIANO. Después de más de sesenta años en Argentina el acta lo sigue registrando de nacionalidad italiana: nunca se nacionalizó.
  • EL DECLARANTE NO ERA DE LA FAMILIA. Sixto Gómez «obra en virtud de su persona autorizada para tal fin»: es un gestor, casi con seguridad de la funeraria. Berutti 5663 es SU domicilio, no el de los Faienza.

Portada
«ANNO 1899 · Comune di PAGLIETA · Provincia di CHIETI · INDICE ANNUALE del REGISTRO degli Atti di NASCITA»
Alcance
el índice cubre el año entero, incluidas las actas de la parte II, que van marcadas «2ª P.». El número de acta más alto que aparece es el 161

What the manuscript says

LA LETRA F ENTERA, tal como está en la página 117:
  Florio (di) Antonio ............ 1
  Fazia (fu) Domenico ............ 8
  Florio (di) Sebastiano ......... 15
  Falcis (di) Carmela ............ 42
  Florio (di) Antonio ............ 65
  Formica Pietro Nicola .......... 68
  Florio (di) Antonia ............ 109
  Felice (di) Etterina Maria Nicola  124
  Fabrizio Verina ................ 160
Y después, directamente, la G. Nueve apellidos con F y ninguno es FAIENZA.

Translation

THERE IS NO RECORD TO TRANSCRIBE: this is a negative result, and it is filed as such.

The complete alphabetical index of the Paglieta birth register for 1899 was downloaded — the cover and all three pages of entries — and read letter by letter. The F occupies nine lines, all legible, and none of them says Faienza. Nor does the surname appear under any other letter of the index, or spelled any other way (Faenza, Fajenza).

The index leaves no gap: it is the register's official annual index, it covers records numbered 1 to 161 and also includes those of part II, marked «2ª P.». So at Paglieta, in 1899, no Faienza was born.

What you need to know about this record

  • NEGATIVO FIRME, Y ES LA MITAD DEL ENCARGO. En el índice de nacimientos de Paglieta de 1899 NO HAY NINGÚN FAIENZA. Ni con los padres correctos ni con otros: no hay ninguno.
  • EL PUEBLO NUNCA ESTUVO PROBADO. «Paglieta» venía del mismo bloque familiar sin una sola cita que ya había errado dos veces comprobadas. Lo que convierte un hallazgo en prueba no es el pueblo: son los padres. Y los padres han aparecido, pero en otro sitio.
  • DÓNDE HAY QUE BUSCARLO AHORA: EN ATESSA. La nota al margen de la partida de nacimiento de su madre dice que Angiolina Pizzi casó con Nicola Faienza en 1892, acta 101, EN EL REGISTRO DE ATESSA. La familia materna es de Atessa desde 1730. El nacimiento del hijo hay que pedirlo a los Nati de Atessa de 1899 y de 1900.
  • Y HAY UNA RAZÓN PARA MIRAR 1900 ANTES QUE 1899. El registro de cementerio argentino da a Domingo Faienza nacido el 1 de MARZO DE 1900 y muerto el 13 de diciembre de 1985 con 85 años, y las dos cifras cuadran entre sí. Si esa fecha es buena, su acta no está en 1899 en ningún pueblo. Paglieta 1900 no existe en Antenati —cero registros, comprobado—, pero ATESSA 1900 sí debería existir: la serie de Atessa es de las largas.
  • UN DETALLE CURIOSO DEL ÍNDICE DE PAGLIETA: sí hay un «PIZZI Bambino», acta 142. Un recién nacido apellidado Pizzi, sin nombre de pila en el índice. No es nuestro Domenico —el apellido es el de la madre, no el del padre—, pero deja constancia de que el apellido Pizzi también circula por Paglieta.
  • COSTE DE ESTE NEGATIVO: cinco imágenes. Es lo que vale leer un año entero de golpe cuando el registro trae índice al final.

Alcance
el año completo, incluidas las actas de la PARTE II, que van marcadas «p. II» dentro de la misma lista. Número de acta más alto indexado: 396
Actas abiertas

Cómo se recorrió
la parte I llega hasta la página ~270 a razón de 1,42 actas por imagen; sondeos en las páginas 200 (acta 297), 240 (acta 354) y 279 (ya parte II) fijaron la recta. Doce imágenes para cerrar un año de 396 nacimientos

What the manuscript says

LA LETRA F ENTERA DEL ÍNDICE DE ATESSA 1899, tal como está en la página 297:
  FAIENZA Antonio .............. 382
  FAIENZA Carmine .............. 383
  FAIENZA Luzio ................ 215
  FAIENZA Rosina ............... 139
  Falcone · Falcucci · Fanelli · Fantauzzi · Fantini · Fania · Favorito ·
  Fiori · Fidelibus · Filamarino · Fiorito · Flocco · De Francesco · Fritti
y a continuación, directamente, la G.

Y LO QUE DICEN LAS CUATRO ACTAS:
  Actas 382 y 383 — gemelos, Antonio y Carmine, nacidos el 18 de diciembre de
  1899 a las 22:15 y 22:30 en la contrada Piazzano. «È comparso MICHELE FAIENZA
  FU CARMINE, di anni quarantatré, contadino, domiciliato in Atessa… da CELESTE
  SERAFINI sua moglie, contadina, seco lui convivente».
  Acta 215 — Luzio, varón, nacido el 4 de junio de 1899 a la 1:15 en la contrada
  Carafella. «È comparso ANTONIO FAIENZA DI PASQUALE, di anni quarantaquattro,
  contadino… da ANTONIA GIAGNORIO sua moglie». Al margen, «morto nel 1899».
  Acta 139 — Rosina, niña, nacida el 4 de abril de 1899 a las 20:15 en la misma
  contrada Carafella. «È comparso VINCENZO FAIENZA DI PASQUALE, di anni
  trentasette, contadino… da BAMBINA ROSSI sua moglie».

Translation

THERE IS NO RECORD TO TRANSCRIBE: this is another negative result, and it is filed as such. But it is a negative of a different kind from Paglieta's, and a good deal stronger.

At Paglieta the surname Faienza did not appear under any letter of the index and that was enough. At Atessa it appears four times, so it was not enough: all four records had to be opened and the parents checked. None of the four children is the child of Nicola Faienza and Angiolina Pizzi. They belong to three other Faienza families of the same village: that of MICHELE FAIENZA FU CARMINE and Celeste Serafini, in the contrada Piazzano, who had twins in December; and those of ANTONIO and VINCENZO FAIENZA DI PASQUALE — brothers, almost certainly, both of the contrada Carafella — each with one child that year.

Our Nicola is «figlio di GIUSEPPE». Not Michele fu Carmine, nor Antonio di Pasquale, nor Vincenzo di Pasquale.

So at Atessa, in 1899, no child of Nicola Faienza and Angiolina Pizzi was born, and certainly no Domenico Faienza of anybody.

What you need to know about this record

  • SEGUNDO NEGATIVO FIRME, Y MÁS SÓLIDO QUE EL PRIMERO. En Atessa 1899 no hay ningún Domenico Faienza —entre «Carmine» y «Luzio» es donde iría, y no está— y, además, ninguno de los cuatro Faienza del año pertenece a nuestra pareja. No es que no aparezca el apellido: es que aparece y se descarta uno por uno.
  • LO QUE ESTA TANDA SÍ HA RESUELTO ES EL PUEBLO. El acta de matrimonio de 1892 declara a Nicola Faienza «nato in Atessa, residente in Atessa», y a su padre Giuseppe residente en Atessa. La familia es de Atessa y no de Paglieta. Lo que queda por acertar no es el sitio, es el año.
  • Y EL AÑO QUE QUEDA ES 1900. Cerrado 1899 en los dos pueblos, la fecha del registro de cementerio argentino —1 de marzo de 1900, coherente con los 85 años que declara al morir en diciembre de 1985— vuelve a ser la hipótesis buena. La búsqueda de «Atessa 1900» en Antenati dio cero registros en su día; conviene repetirla, porque el fondo de Atessa es de los largos y las altas de catálogo son frecuentes, y si sigue sin estar, pedir la partida al Comune di Atessa.
  • UNA MEDIDA ÚTIL QUE DEJA ESTE BARRIDO: el apellido FAIENZA es corriente en Atessa. Tres familias distintas con hijos en un solo año. Eso quiere decir que coincidir en el apellido no prueba nada, y que en la próxima tanda habrá que mirar siempre el nombre del padre: «di Giuseppe». Es exactamente el mismo error que se cometió con Paglieta, sólo que al revés.
  • UN DETALLE QUE VIENE DEL MISMO REGISTRO: el acta 214, justo encima del Faienza Luzio, es de un Iacobitti Antonio con una anotación marginal del consulado que lo da fallecido en Carapachay (Buenos Aires) el 6 de junio de 1957. La emigración de Atessa al Río de la Plata está escrita en los márgenes de estos mismos libros.
  • COSTE DE ESTE NEGATIVO: doce imágenes para un año de 396 nacimientos. Cuatro del índice y del final del volumen, tres de sondeo y cinco de actas.

Translation

Record number eight hundred and fifty-four. At SANTA FE, department of La Capital, on the twenty-seventh day of November nineteen hundred and sixty-seven, at eight fifteen. Before me, SANTIAGO SATALÉ, head of the Registry of Civil Status, there appeared don ALEJO RIESTRA, merchant, resident at Las Heras 3299,

declaring that at THREE THIRTY ON THE TWENTY-FOURTH of the present month, in this Capital, there died doña CRISTINA RIZZO DE SALERNO, of Argentine nationality, BORN IN THIS CITY, aged seventy-four, married, occupation housewife, Civic Registration number 1,529,760, as a consequence of illness, as stated in the medical certificate of Doctor OSCAR BENAZZOLA.

THAT SHE WAS MARRIED TO SETTINO SALERNO.

THAT THE DECEASED WAS THE DAUGHTER OF DON SALVADOR RIZZO AND DOÑA ÁNGELA COSTABILE.

This record having been read, it was signed with me by the informant and by don AURENCIO VILLAFAÑE, resident at Villa Yapeyú, competent witnesses.

What you need to know about this record

  • *** DA SUS PADRES, Y HASTA HOY LOS RIZZO ERAN UNA SOLA PERSONA SUELTA EN TODO EL ÁRBOL. *** SALVADOR RIZZO y ÁNGELA COSTABILE. Y no es un dato aislado: el acta de matrimonio de 1923, cuarenta y cuatro años antes y con otro declarante, da exactamente los mismos dos nombres.
  • COSTABILE ES APELLIDO DEL SUR DE ITALIA, y encaja con que los Salerno vinieran de RENDE, en Cosenza: la colonia calabresa de Santa Fe se casaba entre sí.
  • MURIÓ A LAS TRES Y MEDIA DE LA MADRUGADA. La inscripción se hizo tres días después, y coincide con la fecha de la ficha del cementerio municipal.
  • *** SU AÑO DE NACIMIENTO NO SE PUEDE FIJAR CON LO QUE HAY. *** Aquí declara 74 años (nacida hacia 1893); en su acta de matrimonio de 1923 declaró 23 (nacida hacia 1900). Siete años de diferencia. Haría falta su partida de nacimiento, que según esta acta es de Santa Fe capital.
  • NI EL DECLARANTE NI EL TESTIGO ERAN DE LA FAMILIA — un comerciante de Las Heras y un vecino de Villa Yapeyú. Mismo patrón que en las partidas de los Faienza: gestores, probablemente de la funeraria. Sus domicilios NO son de la familia.
Ancestor

Salvador Rizzo

Father of Cristina Rizzo. He entered the tree on 29 August 2026, through two Argentine certificates forty-four years apart that name him identically.
